====================================================================================================
QUESTION: If the insured dies on June 1, 2024 (during Year 1) and death is not accidental, how much will the beneficiary receive?
CATEGORY: CAT4_MULTIHOP_REASONING

EVIDENCE: During Year 1 (before May 1, 2025): We pay 30% of face amount ($3,000) plus all premiums paid / EXCEPTION - ACCIDENTAL DEATH: If death results from an accident at any time, we pay the full benefit amount immediately, plus all premiums paid.
====================================================================================================

However, since no specific policy is mentioned, this query likely assumes a known policy structure (e.g., from a prior context). The only way to answer this is to retrieve the death benefit amount specified for non-accidental deaths in Year 1 of the policy.
